# About this item

*    Each bolt extractor set is made of high-hardness chrome-molybdenum steel for superior durability and longer life 
*    The compact design lets the stripped bolt extractor fit into tight spaces. Fit where others won't and can't 
*    The internal thread with reverse spiral flutes is designed to bite down for maximum gripping power. The more you turn, the tighter the bolt extractor socket set grips 
*    The standard 1/4 & 3/8 inch square drive and socket adapter make the nut extractor socket set compatible with hand, air, or power tools—the knockout pin for easier removal 
*    The heavy-duty plastic blow-molded carrying case keeps the lug nut extractor organized, with easy storage and carrying 
*    Clear laser-etched markings for easy identification even in low light conditions

## Product description

![Image 69: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
Are you tired of the bolts, nuts, and screws being damaged even if stripped, rusted, or frozen? What will you do? Break it loose with a breaker bar and then fit the new socket with a hammer or a mallet. But this will damage your socket. Thankfully, THINKWORK upgrade impact bolt extractor set is around you to help you to continue to work on your project with ease.

![Image 71: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
Includes 29 pieces of the most used SAE and metric sizes impact bolt extractors.

3/8" Drive: 13mm, 9/16"(14mm), 5/8"(16mm), 17mm, 11/16", 3/4"(19mm), 1/4", 5/16"(8mm), 3/8", 10mm, 7/16"(11mm), 12mm, 1/2" .

1/4" Drive: 8mm, 8.2mm, 8.5mm, 8.7mm, 9mm, 9.2mm, 9.5mm, 10mm, 6mm, 6.2mm, 6.5mm, 6.7mm, 7mm, 7.2mm, 7.5mm, 7.7mm.

![Image 73: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Superior Gripping Power

The internal thread with reverse spiral flutes is designed to bite down to provide maximum gripping power. The more you turn, the tighter the socket grips.

![Image 75: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Standard 1/4 & 3/8 Inch Drive

All the extractors can be used by hand tools or air and power tools.

![Image 77: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Organized Storage

All the extractors are well packed in a heavy-duty blow-molded case for secure storage and easy carry.

![Image 79: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

![Image 81: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

_5 out of 5 stars_

##### Saved the day!!!

**A Lifesaver for My 2022 Tiffin Motorhome!** I was dealing with a severely stripped oil drain plug on my 2022 Tiffin motorhome, and nothing else seemed to work. After failing to remove the plug in the final attempt using vice grips, I was worried I might have to take it to a shop, but Amazon next-day Prime delivery and the THINKWORK set came to the rescue. The extractors in THINKWORK Bolt Extractor Set are well-made and gripped the stripped bolt perfectly. The Service Tech at the Ford service facility must’ve put a bit of extra elbow grease whenever he tightened it on the last oil change. It took substantial effort once the extractor socket was firmly seated, but with a strong pull on a long-handle ratchet, I was able to remove the drain plug without any further damage. VERY IMPRESSED. The different sizes in the set made it easy to find the perfect fit. The only downside I found so far is with the case - it doesn’t appear to be all that durable, and I had to add an extra bit of padding around the sockets to keep them from falling out of place and getting totally disorganized. That was but a minor inconvenience, and I was able to overcome easy enough. If you're dealing with a stubborn bolt, especially something as critical as an oil drain plug on a motorhome, this set is an absolute must-have. It saved me time, money, and a lot of further frustration. Highly recommended!

Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. **A Lifesaver for My 2022 Tiffin Motorhome!**

I was dealing with a severely stripped oil drain plug on my 2022 Tiffin motorhome, and nothing else seemed to work. After failing to remove the plug in the final attempt using vice grips, I was worried I might have to take it to a shop, but Amazon next-day Prime delivery and the THINKWORK set came to the rescue.

The extractors in THINKWORK Bolt Extractor Set are well-made and gripped the stripped bolt perfectly. The Service Tech at the Ford service facility must’ve put a bit of extra elbow grease whenever he tightened it on the last oil change. It took substantial effort once the extractor socket was firmly seated, but with a strong pull on a long-handle ratchet, I was able to remove the drain plug without any further damage. VERY IMPRESSED.

The different sizes in the set made it easy to find the perfect fit.

The only downside I found so far is with the case - it doesn’t appear to be all that durable, and I had to add an extra bit of padding around the sockets to keep them from falling out of place and getting totally disorganized. That was but a minor inconvenience, and I was able to overcome easy enough.

If you're dealing with a stubborn bolt, especially something as critical as an oil drain plug on a motorhome, this set is an absolute must-have. It saved me time, money, and a lot of further frustration.

[![Image 150](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif) Roy Fine](https://www.amazon.com/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AHMXUWYRNW2SOCSM56BKY7BVNF3A) _5 out of 5 stars_ ##### [Saved the day!!!](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/srp/-/R28PUI8H3K7VBR/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8)Reviewed in the United States on August 22, 2024 [Color: 32 Piece](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/B09WMH8KP7/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)   
**A Lifesaver for My 2022 Tiffin Motorhome!**

I was dealing with a severely stripped oil drain plug on my 2022 Tiffin motorhome, and nothing else seemed to work. After failing to remove the plug in the final attempt using vice grips, I was worried I might have to take it to a shop, but Amazon next-day Prime delivery and the THINKWORK set came to the rescue.

The extractors in THINKWORK Bolt Extractor Set are well-made and gripped the stripped bolt perfectly. The Service Tech at the Ford service facility must’ve put a bit of extra elbow grease whenever he tightened it on the last oil change. It took substantial effort once the extractor socket was firmly seated, but with a strong pull on a long-handle ratchet, I was able to remove the drain plug without any further damage. VERY IMPRESSED.

The different sizes in the set made it easy to find the perfect fit.

The only downside I found so far is with the case - it doesn’t appear to be all that durable, and I had to add an extra bit of padding around the sockets to keep them from falling out of place and getting totally disorganized. That was but a minor inconvenience, and I was able to overcome easy enough.

If you're dealing with a stubborn bolt, especially something as critical as an oil drain plug on a motorhome, this set is an absolute must-have. It saved me time, money, and a lot of further frustration.

Highly recommended!

*   [![Image 156](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif) Kenneth G.](https://www.amazon.com/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AH3N53R5HGQ3YIOYIBR2KB6ODFJA?ref=cm_cr_dp_d_bdcrb_top) _5 out of 5 stars_ ##### [Don't be afraid to purchase this set!!](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/srp/-/RGCAX1GURXHSS/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8)Reviewed in the United States on March 14, 2024 [Color: 32 Piece](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/B09WMH8KP7/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)  Brief content visible, double tap to read full content. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. I am replacing brakes all around on my 91 XJ which includes my wheel cylinders for the back drum brakes. I did a whole replacement of everything about 5 years ago and living in the Northeast rust belt especially on the coast is murder on all bolts and fasteners. I was trying to take out the bolts fastening the wheel cylinder and the edges of the bolt just disintegrated with one turn of the wrench. So I go to my local auto parts store and of course they didn't have any bolt extractors that I needed and just for one 3/8 extractor would have cost over 10 bucks. I looked up Amazon and found this set. I read the reviews and almost all were positive. I recieved it this morning and in less than 5 minutes I had both bolts out. The only issue for me (which has nothing to do with the extractor set)was the space I was limited to tapping on the extractor to the bolt between the leaf spring and the backing plate which is no more than 9 inches. This set is not cheap by any means. They even have a gripping ring around the extractor if you need to use a pair of vise grips. I had an extractor set from a very well known manufacturer and had paid almost 200 dollars over 10 years ago. Unfortunately they were stolen and I have to say this set worked just as great as the other set.

Some people had mentioned about the case and when you get them they are scattered around inside the case which happened in my case as well,this is an easy fix. Just cut out a piece of thick cardboard to fit and using the foam piece it came with I shook it violently and everything stayed in place, but for the price I paid, I wouldn't care if they came in and plastic sandwich bag!! Lol.

Just a little advice for anyone who doesn't have experience with extractors, if you PB blasted the bolts/nuts, just make sure that you hit it with a generous amount of brake cleaner. You want a clean and dry surface on the bolt/nut to minimize any slippage and loss of grip. I can't see how anyone could give this set anything less than 5 stars. I highly recommend this set. For what you pay, you can't go wrong.       [Read more Read less](javascript:void(0))       4 people found this helpful Helpful  Sending feedback...Thank you for your feedback. ##### A must-have tool kit for anyone

Reviewed in Canada on July 1, 2023 [Color: 32 Piece](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/B09WMH8KP7/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)  Brief content visible, double tap to read full content. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. I thought I had to buy new rear lenses for my car because last time I tried to remove them, the 4 nuts holding each rear lights lens, were so tight that the hex nuts stripped and I was thinking that the only way to remove them was to cut each bolt off...this if course would have meant new lense replacement at a cost of $400 each.

But then I came across this remover kit and boy am I glad that I did because it saved me well over $800 in unnecessary expenses.

What I like about this set is that a lot of good thinking has gone into designing their sizes because they go up in functions of millimeter which means you have complete control over badly damaged hex nuts and bolts , regardless of the severity. In my case, some nuts were completely rounded off, with minimal material left, yet I could pick just the right size from the set and easily remove each.

They are also good for removing tightly turned undamaged nuts and bolts as the socket digs into the sides of the hex and effortlessly rotates them loose without slipping and damaging the Hex

I definitely recommend the set.       [Read more Read less](javascript:void(0))

## About this Item

*   Each bolt extractor set is made of high-hardness chrome-molybdenum steel for superior durability and longer life
*   The compact design lets the stripped bolt extractor fit into tight spaces. Fit where others won't and can't
*   The internal thread with reverse spiral flutes is designed to bite down for maximum gripping power. The more you turn, the tighter the bolt extractor socket set grips
*   The standard 1/4 & 3/8 inch square drive and socket adapter make the nut extractor socket set compatible with hand, air, or power tools—the knockout pin for easier removal
*   The heavy-duty plastic blow-molded carrying case keeps the lug nut extractor organized, with easy storage and carrying
*   Clear laser-etched markings for easy identification even in low light conditions

## Product Description

 Looking for a reliable and effective set of automotive tools that can handle any mechanic job? Look no further than our bolt extractor kit, specifically designed to help you tackle stubborn bolts and lug nuts that just won't budge. This high-quality bolt extractor set comes with everything you need to get the job done right, including a variety of extractor bits and sockets to fit any size bolt. Our mechanics tools are made from sturdy and high-quality materials, ensuring that they'll last for years of use and provide consistent performance. With a stripped bolt extractor and stripped bolt remover included in the set, you'll be able to remove even the toughest bolts with ease. And our lug nut remover is perfect for taking off those pesky lug nuts that seem impossible to remove without damaging your rims. So if you're looking for a set of automotive tools that can help you get the job done faster, easier, and with less frustration, look no further than our bolt extractor kit. Whether you're a professional mechanic or just a DIY enthusiast, this extractor set is a must-have for your toolbox. Order yours today and see just how much easier mechanic work can be!
